12cd4a0148de5392adc0dad6dab5e217502d0371: $$\{ x_t \}_{t \in [\epsilon, T]}$$

a846ef9989cd1f39fd234d7ba994bfebed725e72: $$\forall x_t$$

bec4063ba940a8969c9e311aaf913e7e6e90e78e: $$f(x_t,t) \to x_\epsilon$$

e89229d040046af3686f6b9665b9438405edd08b: $$f(x_\epsilon, \epsilon)=x_\epsilon$$

eae246088ece67128acedbd257edba55f593bc26: $$x_\epsilon \sim p_{\text{data}}$$

a4ecb1f6428c78f8eb05d81897da5b621db5a19f: $$x_T \sim p_{\text{prior}}$$

cd6ae831cad9265f8842da15c3925aa54612e8fe: $$\hat{\mathbf{x}}_{t_n}^{\phi} := \mathbf{x}_{t_{n+1}} + \left(t_n - t_{n+1}\right)\Phi\left(\mathbf{x}_{t_{n+1}}, t_{n+1}; \phi\right)$$

e38b44b38a4e2d40f8919eab487cafbed307958e: $$d( f_\theta(x_{t_{n+1}}), f_{\theta^\star}(\hat x_{t_n}^{\phi}) )$$

a6fbc73cf1356624dc24035a967d69629e4b6b7a: $$\mathbf{x}_{t_{n+1}} \sim \mathcal{N}\left(\mathbf{x}; t_{n+1}^{2}\mathbf{I}\right)$$

ec94c266b84c0392099c27ac63daa5a70a213fd8: $$\theta^\star \leftarrow \mu \theta^\star + (1-\mu) \theta$$

023d74640f3fbe476d45d1556c6b8abad9940105: $$\epsilon=t_1 < t_2 < \ldots < t_{N-1}=1$$

e0dabc703b73f4f66353354237f035afda5d6585: $$\begin{aligned}
&\textbf{Input: } \mathcal{D},\theta,\eta,\Phi(\cdot,\cdot;\phi),d,\lambda,\mu,\phi \\
&\theta^\star \leftarrow \theta \\
&\textbf{repeat} \\
&\quad \mathbf{x}\sim\mathcal{D},\quad n\sim\mathcal{U}\{1,\ldots,N-1\} \\
&\quad \mathbf{x}_{t_{n+1}}\sim\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{x};t_{n+1}^2\mathbf{I}) \\
&\quad \hat{\mathbf{x}}_{t_n}^{\phi}\leftarrow\mathbf{x}_{t_{n+1}}+(t_n-t_{n+1})\Phi(\mathbf{x}_{t_{n+1}},t_{n+1};\phi) \\
&\quad \mathcal{L}(\theta,\theta^\star;\phi)\leftarrow\lambda(t_n)d\left(f_\theta(\mathbf{x}_{t_{n+1}},t_{n+1}),f_{\theta^\star}(\hat{\mathbf{x}}_{t_n}^{\phi},t_n)\right) \\
&\quad \theta\leftarrow\theta-\eta\nabla_\theta\mathcal{L}(\theta,\theta^\star;\phi) \\
&\quad \theta^\star\leftarrow\operatorname{stopgrad}\left(\mu\theta^\star+(1-\mu)\theta\right) \\
&\textbf{until convergence}
\end{aligned}$$

58c7f580048f4461e49735845444591ba75f3c07: $$f(\cdot, \cdot)=0$$

aafce8c5b0f3e858db889bf74470b4f19184f942: $$\begin{aligned}
&\textbf{Input: } \text{dataset } \mathcal{D},\ \theta,\ \eta,\ N(\cdot), \mu(\cdot),\ d(\cdot,\cdot),\ \text{and } \lambda(\cdot) \\
&\theta^\star \leftarrow \theta,\quad k \leftarrow 0 \\
&\textbf{repeat} \\
&\quad \text{Sample } \mathbf{x}\sim\mathcal{D},\quad n\sim\mathcal{U}\{ 1,N(k)-1 \} \\
&\quad \text{Sample } \mathbf{z}\sim\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{0},\mathbf{I}) \\
&\quad \mathcal{L}(\theta,\theta^\star)\leftarrow
\lambda(t_n)d\left(
f_\theta(\mathbf{x}+t_{n+1}\mathbf{z},t_{n+1}),
f_{\theta^\star}(\mathbf{x}+t_n\mathbf{z},t_n)
\right) \\
&\quad \theta\leftarrow\theta-\eta\nabla_\theta\mathcal{L}(\theta,\theta^\star) \\
&\quad \theta^\star\leftarrow\operatorname{stopgrad}\left(\mu(k)\theta^\star+\left(1-\mu(k)\right)\theta\right) \\
&\quad k\leftarrow k+1 \\
&\textbf{until convergence}
\end{aligned}$$

2a4517a495cb8d0637b90ac2d829e019e62a02ef: $$\nabla \log p_t(\mathbf{x}_t)
=
-\mathbb{E}\left[
\left.
\frac{\mathbf{x}_t-\mathbf{x}}{t^2}
\right|
\mathbf{x}_t
\right]$$

7bb1701006e9d5ed384c63b8cfbdcdb9dd1049ca: $$\mathcal{L}_{CD}^{N}(\theta,\theta^{\star};\phi)
=
\mathcal{L}_{CT}^{N}(\theta,\theta^{\star})
+
o(\Delta t)$$

5ec8281133d9624c07d9a562de3398154d36b601: $$\Delta t := \max_{n\in \{1,N-1 \}}\left\{\left|t_{n+1}-t_n\right|\right\}$$

1b8a3392e2486d24e9f87351daed6fe6a1b0b1a9: $$\mathcal{L}(\theta,\theta^\star) =
\lambda(t_n)d\left(
f_\theta(\mathbf{x}+t_{n+1}\mathbf{z},t_{n+1}),
f_{\theta^\star}(\mathbf{x}+t_n\mathbf{z},t_n)
\right)$$

b648828fcd8e95b521eba4fc383f8316dc741339: $$\mathbb{E}_{x_t,t}\left[

\right]$$
